    9. Differentiate between gender identity and sexual orientation.
    Answer:

    Gender identity refers to a person's internal understanding and experience of their gender.

    Sexual orientation refers to a person's pattern of romantic, emotional, or sexual attraction towards others.

    Both are important aspects of human identity, but they describe different experiences.

    10. What is patriarchy?
    Answer:

    Patriarchy is a social system in which men traditionally hold greater authority and power than women in family, society, politics, and other institutions.

    In media, patriarchal ideology may appear through the presentation of men as dominant decision-makers and women as dependent or subordinate individuals.

    11. How can films promote gender equality?
    Answer:

    Films can promote gender equality by presenting independent women, supportive men, equal relationships, and diverse gender identities.

    They can challenge patriarchal traditions, highlight women's achievements, and raise awareness about discrimination. Through meaningful stories and characters, films can encourage social change and equal opportunities.

    12. Explain the role of advertisements in promoting gender equality.
    Answer:

    Advertisements can promote gender equality by showing men and women as equally capable and responsible.

    They can present men sharing household duties, women working in leadership positions, and people challenging traditional gender roles. Such representations encourage audiences to develop more progressive attitudes.

    13. What is body positivity?
    Answer:

    Body positivity is an approach that encourages people to accept and respect their bodies regardless of shape, size, skin colour, or physical appearance.

    It challenges narrow beauty standards and promotes self-confidence, diversity, and respect for individual differences.

    14. What is media literacy?
    Answer:

    Media literacy is the ability to access, understand, evaluate, and critically examine media messages.

    It helps individuals identify stereotypes, recognize misleading information, understand media techniques, and make informed decisions about the content they consume.

    15. What is feminist film theory?
    Answer:

    Feminist film theory is an approach to studying cinema that examines the representation of women, gender relations, power structures, and patriarchal ideology in films.

    It questions traditional stereotypes and analyzes how films may present women as objects of visual pleasure rather than independent individuals.

    16. What is the importance of LGBTQ+ representation in media?
    Answer:

    LGBTQ+ representation is important because it gives visibility to diverse gender identities and sexual orientations.

    Positive representation can increase awareness, challenge stereotypes, reduce prejudice, and encourage social acceptance. It also allows underrepresented communities to see their experiences reflected respectfully in media.

    17. How do advertisements influence gender identity?
    Answer:

    Advertisements influence gender identity by presenting particular expectations about appearance, behaviour, lifestyle, and social roles.

    Traditional advertisements may associate women with beauty and household responsibilities and men with authority and financial success. Inclusive advertisements can challenge these expectations and promote diverse identities.

    18. Explain the relationship between gender and consumer culture.
    Answer:

    Gender and consumer culture are connected because advertisements often use gender identities and stereotypes to promote products.

    Beauty products, fashion, technology, and household goods may be marketed according to traditional ideas about masculinity and femininity.

    Critical analysis helps audiences understand how commercial interests can influence gender expectations and consumer behaviour.

    19. What is responsible representation in media?
    Answer:

    Responsible representation means presenting individuals and communities fairly, respectfully, and without harmful stereotypes.

    It includes equal opportunities, diverse characters, accurate representation, respect for privacy, and the avoidance of discrimination and objectification.

    20. What is intersectionality?
    Answer:

    Intersectionality is a concept that explains how different forms of social identity and inequality interact with one another.

    For example, an individual may experience discrimination based on gender as well as class, caste, disability, or other social positions.

    It helps media researchers understand the diverse experiences of individuals and communities.

    21. Discuss the role of media in social change.
    Answer:

    Media can contribute to social change by spreading awareness, challenging traditional beliefs, and encouraging positive attitudes.

    Films and advertisements can highlight gender discrimination, promote women's empowerment, support equal opportunities, and present diverse identities.

    Through responsible representation, media can encourage audiences to question inequality and support a more inclusive society.

    22. Explain the harmful effects of gender stereotypes in media.
    Answer:

    Gender stereotypes in media create fixed expectations about how men and women should behave.

    They may restrict individual freedom, discourage people from choosing certain careers, reduce self-confidence, and reinforce unequal social relationships.

    Challenging these stereotypes is essential for promoting dignity, equality, and personal freedom.

    23. Why is critical analysis of films and advertisements important?
    Answer:

    Critical analysis helps audiences understand how media communicates ideas about gender, power, identity, and society.

    It reveals stereotypes, objectification, discrimination, and hidden ideological messages. It also develops media literacy and encourages responsible media consumption.

    24. How can media promote positive masculinity?
    Answer:

    Media can promote positive masculinity by presenting men as caring, respectful, emotionally expressive, and supportive individuals.

    It can show men sharing domestic responsibilities, supporting women's independence, and rejecting violence and discrimination. Such representations challenge restrictive masculine stereotypes.

    25. Discuss the importance of gender equality in contemporary media.
    Answer:

    Gender equality in contemporary media is important because it promotes fair representation and challenges traditional stereotypes.

    It provides visibility to different identities, encourages equal opportunities, and supports women's empowerment. Inclusive films and advertisements can influence public attitudes and contribute to a more respectful and equal society.
